# Changed test expectations — R22 and R23 Phases E/F/F3

Baseline: `8c456709`. This inventory covers every changed pre-existing test file from `git diff 8c456709 -- '*test*' '*tests*'`, plus changed JSON fixtures directly consumed by those tests. New test files are not pre-existing expectations.

Counts: **23 test files**, **135 assertion or test-input changes**, **2 consumed JSON fixtures**, **616 semantic fixture changes**; **750 JUSTIFIED**, **1 UNJUSTIFIED / RESTORED**, **0 FLAGGED**.

Each row identifies a changed assertion or an unchanged assertion whose fixture/input or resolver changed. Added assertions in pre-existing files are included. Imports, comments and test-title edits alone are not assertion changes. Old/main line numbers are supplied where the expression itself changed; fixture-driven rows point to the current consuming assertion.

R22 restored execution isolation in Core enrichment and catalog normalization. The two PR-added Core modality tests were corrected to assert specifications.modalityFacts instead of requiring top-level execution modalities to equal display facts. Those tests did not exist at 8c456709 and therefore do not inflate the pre-existing-file count. No pre-existing display assertion became unnecessary merely by restoring execution isolation.

R6A had an unjustified blanket exception derived from archived full-object output. It is removed: ordinary retention assertions are restored, and only twelve explicitly pinned schema omissions across nine identities remain allowed. Their existing source records and dates are checked directly. No unresolved FLAGGED expectations remain.

The frozen execution fixture is generated by scripts/freeze-execution-capability.mjs from git show 8c456709 through an in-memory loader. It covers every static identity and every full-registry identity in the offline scenario, all execution fields outside the four display projections, catalog normalization, Core reference modes, Web vision/refine decisions, and real Mini settings/index decisions. Four new per-end tests compare current outputs to this fixture.

The picker-name fixture and tests are preserved: all 1064 static names must equal main. Existing historical fixture JSON files under tests/fixtures are unchanged. Newly added R21/phase-2 evidence and report JSON, the name fixture, the R22 execution fixture, and the twelve-case schema fixture are new files rather than edits to pre-existing fixtures.

All evidence references are local archived records or code rules. This review made no network requests and does not newly verify channel availability, account entitlement, model quality or HTTP status.

R22 Phase B retains the Phase A review ledger. Rows marked Phase B compare c76e9c2a to the current worktree; earlier rows are historical decisions, and overlapping edits are marked superseded. Whole-file diff hashes and the required file inventory still use main 8c456709. No historical evidence fixture is rewritten. New-since-main R21 tests also switch only their seven link expectations and displayed modality order; the new Phase B tests independently check all 74 pages, all 1064 source lists, vendor labels and Docker COPY layouts.

R22 Phase C preserves fact/unknown, execution, name, source and selection assertions and both Mini byte ceilings. Five main-existing test files update presentation expectations: channel wording, decorative question badges, keyboard traversal of evidence controls and selecting the named Backup action after adding evidence buttons. Two expectations introduced after main are separately hashed against 2550c736: R21 Qwen accessible names and R22B accessible-name order rather than tooltip titles. Each change below gives its reason. The Mini build-script change is also reviewed. New Phase C tests independently check state distinctions, per-identity captured evidence, mobile/compact dialogs and non-colour contrast.

R23 Phase E: review diffSha256 values now hash length-delimited old/new file bytes, independent of core.abbrev, Git diff formatting or configuration. Frozen inventory content hashes and history-free tamper checks remain active. The five Mini single-source/notice expectations and the phase-2 contract fixture changes below preserve their original primary links, all 1061 Core card checks and both byte ceilings. The four execution locks and history-free loader now decompress the same frozen JSON bytes; no execution expectation changed. Picker and audit-helper generated JS are compacted after tsc to preserve package budgets.

R23 Phase F retains all execution, picker-name, source URL/order/role, layout, value and modality-state assertions. Source-label expectations now include the unchanged curated card name or a readable page subject with numeric separators. The exact historical TokenDance hint changes to 渠道页无此型号. Branch-added Mini R22B/R22C expectations are reviewed against 1dcd8b13; existing follow-up bases remain intact. New client scans cover all 1064 cards, all Web evidence/notice dialogs and all Mini copy callbacks; both source byte ceilings are unchanged. Saved-ID/full-model equality is preserved in production code, without weakening that test.

R23 Phase F3 adds catalog-backed dotted-version controls to both existing client scan tests and reviews the independent shared heuristic against fd29cf36. All pre-existing client assertions remain. Genuine known model hyphens are valid, and parameter tokens such as 31B are no longer mistaken for split versions. Known dotted corrections may differ from a slug-like picker label only in source titles. Both byte ceilings and all 1064 identity/name/URL assertions remain unchanged. Additional F3 cases retain complete BFL brackets and dotted FLUX.1.1 spelling, ignore escaped Markdown candidates on general pages, and keep short IDs from relabeling another model family.

R23 Phase H2 corrects two stale branch-added Core/Gateway channel-only expectations. Their files are byte-identical at fd29cf36 and operator HEAD c75aa00f, so the existing follow-up baseline applies. Expected appended URLs are derived from vendor-provenance fields (the fields labelled 源自原厂), with canonical deduplication, channel-first order, independent portal/OpenAPI/JSON rejection and 原厂规格 labels. Explicit deepseek-v4.1-flash and qwen3.6-plus cases tie the appended URL to maxOutputTokens. No product code, execution expectation or historical fallback changes; two follow-up entries are recorded separately from the main-existing counts.

## Verification

`node scripts/report-changed-tests.mjs --check` independently recomputes the changed pre-existing file list, checks directly consumed JSON fixtures, checks every old/new blob-content hash (independent of Git diff formatting and core.abbrev) and review count, and verifies this Markdown matches the JSON. A negative test removes a reviewed file and requires the guard to fail.
